Bacterial growth curve fitting (logistic, Gompertz, Baranyi), Lotka-Volterra community dynamics, Gillespie stochastic simulation, biofilm quantification, CFU enumeration, and genome annotation. For metabolic modeling use cobrapy; for sequence analysis use biopython. ([synthetic-sciences/openscience](https://github.com/synthetic-sciences/openscience/tree/HEAD/backend/cli/skills/biology/microbial-dynamics) · ★4.4k · 9 copies)
- **microbial-ecologist**: Think and work like an expert Microbial Ecologist. Use when a task calls for Microbial Ecologist judgment. - **microbiome**: Read before you analyze amplicon or shotgun microbiome data (ASVs, taxonomy, diversity, differential abundance). The data is compositional, thus usual tests and RNA-seq methods give false results. It gives the correct method for each design and the rules for rarefaction and zeros. ([inflexa-ai/inflexa](https://github.com/inflexa-ai/inflexa/tree/HEAD/skills/microbiome) · ★38)
- **Microbiome Scientist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Microbiome Scientist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-microbiome-scientist))
- **microbiome-abundance-table-model-evaluation**: Use when you have a microbial abundance table (OTU or species table) plus sample disease/status labels and need to train and benchmark a phylogeny-aware deep-learning classifier for microbiome status or disease detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-abundance-table-model-evaluation))
- **microbiome-alpha-diversity-boxplot-visualization**: Use when you have aggregated alpha diversity tables (alpha-out_dataset-name.tsv or alpha_aggregated.tsv) with sample metadata and need to compare metric distributions between microbiome projects, e.g. reproducing Fig. 4 for the 471_swedish and 514_imp_project cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-alpha-diversity-boxplot-visualization))
- **microbiome-alpha-diversity-marker-combination**: Use when you have effect estimates and standard errors per study and marker (≥10 studies), want a global conclusion on 'α-diversity' across multiple related markers, and have no prior marker-by-marker correlation estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-alpha-diversity-marker-combination))
- **microbiome-amplicon**: Workflow for amplicon microbiome analysis including denoising, taxonomy assignment, diversity analysis, and differential abundance. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioclaw_hub/microbiome-amplicon) · ★224 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-amplicon-processing**: Amplicon sequence variant (ASV) inference from 16S rRNA or ITS amplicon sequencing using DADA2. Covers quality filtering, error learning, denoising, and chimera removal. Use when processing demultiplexed amplicon FASTQ files to generate an ASV table for downstream analysis. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-amplicon-processing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-analysis**: 16S and microbiome data analysis — denoising, diversity, differential abundance, and compositional methods. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/microbiome-analysis) · ★68 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-analysis-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `Microbiome analysis` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics-and-other-omics/microbiome-analysis-starter) · ★46)
- **microbiome-beta-diversity-jaccard-computation**: Use when a processed microbiome OTU table (e.g., otu.tab.txt or a phyloseq object such as biom.Rdata) must be converted into a Jaccard dissimilarity matrix for beta-diversity analysis or survival association testing in the MiSurv/MiRKAT workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-beta-diversity-jaccard-computation))
- **microbiome-beta-diversity-ordination**: Use when a paired microbiome/metabolomics study needs a genotype- or treatment-level test of community composition from a compositional feature table (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-beta-diversity-ordination))
- **microbiome-biomarker-clade-highlighting**: Use when you need to render LEfSe biomarker results as a compact circular phylogenetic cladogram, e.g., highlighting taxonomic clades enriched in one cohort (HMP) versus another (MetaHIT) from MetaPhlAn taxonomic profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-biomarker-clade-highlighting))
- **microbiome-body-site-clustering**: Use when you have paired-end WGS or simulated long-read metagenome samples with known body-site labels and need to compare how k-mer-based tools cluster them by origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-body-site-clustering))

- **microbiome-cancer-agent**: AI-powered analysis of microbiome-cancer interactions including tumor microbiome profiling, immunotherapy response prediction, and microbiome-targeted therapeutic opportunities. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-cancer-agent) · ★104 · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-cancer-agent**: ---name: microbiome-cancer-agent ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/analysis/microbiome-cancer-agent) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-community-composition-validation**: Use when you have a DADA2-denoised QIIME 2 feature table from a Tourmaline run with sample metadata (e.g. 5.0-um vs 0.22-um size fractions) and must verify which taxa dominate each fraction before publishing or reusing the findings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-community-composition-validation))
- **microbiome-community-visualization-phyloseq**: Use when quality-filtered 16S rRNA amplicon reads have been processed into OTU (97% identity, mothur) or ASV (DADA2 denoising) tables with taxonomy assignments, and taxonomic composition visualizations are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-community-visualization-phyloseq))
- **microbiome-compositional-data-analysis**: Use when evaluating which DA method controls FDR at a nominal level (e.g. 5%) while maintaining power on compositional microbiome count data, or when reproducing simulation-based method comparisons across balanced and unbalanced group designs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-compositional-data-analysis))
- **microbiome-count-normalization**: Use when raw OTU/feature count tables need normalization prior to ordination, BSS, or differential abundance analysis, and competing normalization methods must be ranked on a dataset such as the global gut microbiota data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-count-normalization))
- **microbiome-count-normalization-css-tmm-quantile**: Use when a merged MetaPhlAn or QIIME OTU/BIOM taxonomy table needs rescaling across samples before GLM, Random Forest, or SVM model building, or when comparing how normalization choices affect classifier accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-count-normalization-css-tmm-quantile))
- **microbiome-count-normalization-undersampling-correction**: Use when calling differentially abundant features (OTUs, species, or other taxa) across two or more sample groups from sparse, undersampled microbial count matrices where library-size normalization biases dominate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-count-normalization-undersampling-correction))
- **microbiome-curation**: Skill for curating microbiome-related pathophysiology in the dismech knowledge base. Use this skill when adding dysbiosis mechanisms, ecological concepts (Anna Karenina, keystone taxa, colonization resistance), SCFA/metabolite pathways, and linking microbial ecology to disease pathophysiology. Covers IBD, C. diff, obesity, and other microbiome-associated conditions. ([monarch-initiative/dismech](https://github.com/monarch-initiative/dismech/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/microbiome-curation) · ★73)
- **microbiome-cutotype-stratification**: Use when you have a species-level microbial abundance table plus sample metadata with group labels (e.g., cutotypes) and want genus-level enrichment of taxa rather than KEGG pathway/functional enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-cutotype-stratification))
- **microbiome-dataset-characteristic-computation**: Use when you have per-tool significant ASV outputs for many two-group 16S datasets and want to test whether the numbers of significant features correlate with effect size, sample size, sequencing depth, or sparsity, with vs without prevalence filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-dataset-characteristic-computation))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance**: Differential abundance testing for microbiome data using compositionally-aware methods like ALDEx2, ANCOM-BC2, and MaAsLin2. Use when identifying taxa that differ between experimental groups while accounting for the compositional nature of microbiome data. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-differential-abundance) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-analysis**: Use when analyzing microbiome OTU/feature count tables to identify taxa differentially abundant across experimental groups (e.g., nationality, age strata, bmi_group) while accounting for compositionality and unequal sampling fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-analysis))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-results-aggregation**: Use when you have significant-ASV result tables from several DA tools (e.g., 14 tools) run on the same datasets and need to quantify overlap, consensus, or tool clustering, including effects of prevalence filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-results-aggregation))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when comparing compositional microbiome feature counts between AD model and control cohorts, e.g. testing KEGG pathway abundances in the 3xTg Sacrifice cohort against WT to reproduce Figure 5E. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-testing))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when a binary or continuous grouping factor and a microbiome feature table are available and you need to test differential abundance while quantifying robustness of individual features and the overall microbiome-group association. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-testing))
- **microbiome-disease-research**: 研究微生物组与疾病机制证据链并形成研究假设。适用于：梳理菌群、代谢物、宿主通路与疾病表型之间的证据链，区分相关性与因果性并形成可验证研究假设。 ([patsnap/skills](https://github.com/patsnap/skills/tree/HEAD/open-platform/microbiome-disease-research) · ★46)
- **microbiome-diversity-analysis**: Alpha and beta diversity analysis for microbiome data. Calculate within-sample richness, evenness, and between-sample dissimilarity with phyloseq and vegan. Use when comparing community composition across samples or testing for group differences in microbiome structure. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-diversity-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-diversity-report-component-organization**: Use when you have raw microbiome analysis outputs (e.g., EMP data, alpha diversity, Shannon entropy, network files) that must be assembled into a reproducible structured scientific report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-diversity-report-component-organization))
- **microbiome-diversity-reporter**: Interpret Alpha and Beta diversity metrics from 16S rRNA sequencing results. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Academic%20Writing/microbiome-diversity-reporter) · ★2.1k)
- **microbiome-diversity-statistics**: Use when a genus-level ASV abundance table is grouped by experimental condition or amplified region and you must test whether alpha diversity (richness/evenness) differs significantly across groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-diversity-statistics))
- **microbiome-effect-size-visualization**: Use when progressive permutation has flagged robust microbiome features (observed -log10 p-values outside the 95% quantile intervals of the fully permuted scenario) and you need signed effect sizes and individual abundance plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-effect-size-visualization))
- **microbiome-environment-stratification**: Use when an EMP 16S Release 1 BIOM table and mapping file must be analyzed per environment type — e.g., computing NODF nestedness separately for non-saline, saline, animal, and plant subsets of a 2,000-sample subset rather than only globally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-environment-stratification))
- **microbiome-feature-ordering-for-cnn-input**: Use when you have a cophenetic distance matrix from a phylogenetic tree (e.g., from the R ape package) of OTU features and need to reorder/group them phylogenetically before training a CNN-based microbiome classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-ordering-for-cnn-input))
- **microbiome-feature-robustness-ranking**: Use when binary-grouped microbiome count data has yielded significant differential features and you need to rank how robust each discovery is to progressive permutation of grouping labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-robustness-ranking))
- **microbiome-feature-screening**: Use when a high-dimensional OTU/taxa count matrix (e.g. 856 OTUs x 60 samples) and an adjusted response vector must be reduced to a non-empty subset S of taxa likely associated with the outcome, prior to a data-adaptive multivariate association test under a generalized linear model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-screening))
- **microbiome-feature-table-analysis**: Use when an amplicon feature table (BIOM/QIIME 2 format) and a sample metadata mapping file are loaded and you must determine whether experimental groups differ in alpha- or beta-diversity and which features are biomarkers of each group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-table-analysis))
- **microbiome-feature-table-rarefaction**: Use when a feature table (Deblur or OTU BIOM) with uneven per-sample sequence counts feeds into cross-sample statistics (e.g., random forest classification of EMPO environment labels), and per-sample depths must be equalized to 5,000 observations first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-table-rarefaction))
- **microbiome-fragility-index-computation**: Use when a set of differentially abundant microbiome features from a binary-group comparison must be ranked by robustness, by counting how many progressive label-permutation steps flip each feature to nonsignificance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-fragility-index-computation))
- **microbiome-functional-data-interpretation**: Use when input is PICRUSt2 predicted functional output (e.g., KO abundance tables and sample metadata) and the goal is differential pathway abundance, pathway annotation, or GSEA between comparison groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-functional-data-interpretation))
- **microbiome-functional-prediction**: Predict metagenome functional content from 16S rRNA marker gene data using PICRUSt2. Infer KEGG, MetaCyc, and EC abundances from ASV tables. Use when functional profiling is needed from 16S data without shotgun metagenomics sequencing. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-functional-prediction) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-functional-profile-visualization**: Use when you have a DA results table from pathway_daa() applied to PICRUSt2 KEGG pathway abundances and need a publication-quality figure showing which pathways differ between groups and by how much. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-functional-profile-visualization))
- **microbiome-genotype-association-analysis**: Use when you have a SNP genotype matrix and a microbial taxa abundance matrix over the same sample cohort and need to identify significant taxa-SNP (mbQTL) relationships, including all-to-all R² estimates or a single specified rsID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-genotype-association-analysis))
- **microbiome-group-comparison-design**: Use when you have a PICRUSt2-predicted pathway-level abundance table (KO converted to KEGG pathway abundance) plus sample metadata with a grouping variable, and you must test which pathways differ significantly between groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-group-comparison-design))
- **microbiome-group-difference-analysis**: Use when a microbiome OTU/feature count table has a binary grouping factor and you need to characterize overall association strength and the robustness of significant features across progressive label permutations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-group-difference-analysis))
- **microbiome-host-phenotype-association-analysis**: Use when metagenomic shotgun samples have been profiled with MetaPhlAn 4 and you must link taxonomic abundances (e.g. SGB4777) to host markers like HEI, aMED, uPDI or ASCVD risk via prediction metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-host-phenotype-association-analysis))
- **microbiome-library-size-normalization**: Use when preparing 16S rRNA gene ASV/OTU count tables for differential abundance tools that require rarefied input, or when reproducing the rarefaction-vs-no-rarefaction arm of a DA method comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-library-size-normalization))
- **microbiome-marker-gene-survey-analysis**: Use when the input is a sparse high-throughput microbiome OTU/taxa count matrix with two or more sample groups and the goal is to identify features that are differentially abundant across populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-marker-gene-survey-analysis))
- **microbiome-metabolite-prediction-modeling**: Use when paired samples have matched microbiome (taxonomic or functional abundance) and metabolite count tables and the goal is to predict metabolite abundances, identify well-predicted metabolites, or infer microbe-metabolite interaction networks without relying on metabolite annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metabolite-prediction-modeling))
- **microbiome-metabolome-abundance-normalization**: Use when when you have raw count matrices from paired microbiome (16S rRNA or metagenomic) and metabolomic (LC-MS/MS) profiling data that will be used to train or apply a predictive model (e.g., MiMeNet, MelonnPan, Random Forest) to predict metabolite abundances from microbial composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-abundance-normalization) · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-metabolome-cross-validation**: Use when paired microbial-feature and metabolite tables (samples-by-features CSV) must be evaluated by k-fold cross-validation and a principled significance cutoff for well-predicted metabolites is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-cross-validation))
- **microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing**: Use when when starting with raw paired microbiome (16S rRNA, metagenomic taxonomic or functional features) and metabolome (LC-MS/MS, NMR) count tables from the same biospecimens, and planning to train prediction models or co-abundance networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing-clr-transformation**: Use when you have paired microbiome and metabolomic abundance tables (samples × features) with relative abundance or raw count values, and you are preparing data for downstream regression or neural network modeling of microbe-metabolite relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing-clr-transformation) · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-metabolome-integrative-analysis**: Use when paired samples have matched taxonomic/functional abundance and metabolite abundance matrices (e.g. IBD PRISM, cystic fibrosis, soil datasets) and you need to predict the metabolome, identify well-predicted metabolites, and group microbes and metabolites with similar interaction patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-integrative-analysis))
- **microbiome-metabolome-prediction-modeling**: Use when you have paired microbiome (16S rRNA, metagenomic) and metabolomic (LC-MS, GC-MS) abundance tables from the same biosamples, and you want to predict which metabolites are recoverable from microbial composition alone and identify groups of microbes and metabolites with correlated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-prediction-modeling) · 4 copies)
- **microbiome-metadata-association-analysis**: Use when you have per-sample inStrain profile objects (or equivalent strain calls) plus per-infant metadata (sibling/twin relationships, birth day, birth weight, gestational age) and need to test whether strain sharing correlates with these host properties.
